[AppDomain]::CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies() | % { [Runtime.TargetedPatchingOptOut]::IsResourceServiceModule($_) }
Measure-Command { pwsh -NoProfile -Command "exit" }
# This script times your profile loading line-by-line`
$ProfilePath = $PROFILE.CurrentUserAllHosts`
if (Test-Path $ProfilePath) {`
    $lines = Get-Content $ProfilePath`
    foreach ($line in $lines) {`
        if ($line.Trim() -and -not $line.StartsWith("#")) {`
            $time = Measure-Command { Invoke-Expression $line }`
            Write-Host "$($time.TotalMilliseconds)ms : $line"`
        }`
    }`
}
# This script times your profile loading line-by-line
$ProfilePath = $PROFILE.CurrentUserAllHosts
if (Test-Path $ProfilePath) {`
    $lines = Get-Content $ProfilePath`
    foreach ($line in $lines) {`
        if ($line.Trim() -and -not $line.StartsWith("#")) {`
            $time = Measure-Command { Invoke-Expression $line }`
            Write-Host "$($time.TotalMilliseconds)ms : $line"`
        }`
    }`
}

    for (int i = 0; i < G_APP_COUNT; i++) {`
        if (strcmp(G_APPS[i].name, display_name) == 0) {`

# Search for glu libraries on your system
find /mingw64 -name "*glu*" 2>/dev/null
find / -name "libglu32.a" 2>/dev/null
# List all files in mingw64 lib directory
ls /mingw64/lib/*glu* 2>/dev/null
# Or search recursively
